create table address(id int, city varchar(10), foreign key(id) references student(id) on delete cascade);



select * from emp, dept;
select * from emp CROSS JOIN dept;

--------------------------------------

select ename, dname, sal from emp, dept WHERE (emp.deptno = dept.deptno);

select ename, dname, sal from emp, dept WHERE (emp.deptno = dept.deptno) AND sal >=3000;


select ename, dname, sal from emp INNER JOIN dept ON (emp.deptno = dept.deptno);

select ename, dname, sal from emp INNER JOIN dept ON (emp.deptno = dept.deptno) WHERE sal >=3000;




--------------------------------------



select emp.deptno , dept.deptno from emp right  outer join  dept ON emp.deptno = dept.deptno;

mysql> table d;
Empty set (0.00 sec)

mysql> insert into d values(1,1);

mysql> savepoint a;

mysql> insert into d values(2,2);
Query OK, 1 row affected (0.00 sec)

mysql> savepoint b;
Query OK, 0 rows affected (0.00 sec)

mysql> insert into d values(3,3);
Query OK, 1 row affected (0.00 sec)

mysql> select * from d;
+------+------+
| c1   | c2   |
+------+------+
|    1 |    1 |
|    2 |    2 |
|    3 |    3 |
+------+------+
3 rows in set (0.00 sec)

mysql> rollback to b;
Query OK, 0 rows affected (0.00 sec)

mysql> select * from d;

| c1   | c2   |
+------+------+
|    1 |    1 |
|    2 |    2 |
+------+------+
2 rows in set (0.00 sec)

Query OK, 0 rows affected (0.01 sec)
